## 16. Record — the user's decisions

Dated decisions and constraints, quoted as recorded.

#### User decisions — the early record, §1–§10.12 (28 August – 3 September)

No dated, verbatim-quoted user decision appears in lines 2085–3041. User-attributed items: §3.7 "The user's fine-tuned model puts the dimer at −4·10¹² eV, with forces of 10¹⁶ eV/Å at 4 Å"; §10.3 the fine-tune is re-done "on the real hydrogen training set as the user did (20000 steps, batch 4, learning rate 10⁻⁴)", the user's model reaching 0.96 meV/atom and 52.5 meV/Å; §10.4 "The user's observation on such checkpoints — validation force RMSE explodes first, then force MAE, then energy, while the training loss stays normal — is what the baseline should reproduce and the hinge should prevent", and "On the user's own metric, the force RMSE over the full 10% subset (107612 frames) …"; §10.5–10.6 "the user's model was trained for 10⁶ steps (§3.6)" at "a recipe whose learning rate is not recorded".

Project constraints (§1). Goal: a change of the training procedure — preferably a loss term — using nothing but the training set, making the *first* training run (from scratch or in pretraining) produce weights without such features; repairing an already-defective model is a by-product and a check of the mechanism, not the objective. Constraints: no new labels, and no use of validation or test data in any training or repair step meant as a method (the two §8.3 experiments using validation frames as anchors are marked demonstrations); no molecular dynamics inside pretraining; small cost, compatible with mixed precision, no loss of accuracy on the data; analytic short-range repulsion outside scope.

______________________________________________________________________

#### User decisions — the refreshed reference, §10.13 first half (2–6 September)

- No-ZBL rule (in force 5 September): `neo_plain_2gpu_zbl` (E27) "stopped at 15:00, 5 September, about 4000 steps in: withdrawn on the user's no-ZBL rule (ledger E27)"; `neo_plain_2gpu_zone` (E29) "stopped at 15:00, 5 September, at its first batches: withdrawn on the user's no-ZBL rule (ledger E29)".
- Rule of 11:57, 6 September (the short range ZBL will later cover is exempt): E58's twin "stopped 11:57, 6 September: the inequality's range lies inside the region ZBL will cover later, which the user's rule of 11:57 exempts — ledger E58". Applied again at 14:47, 6 September when re-reading `neo_plain_2gpu_gauss_so2knee_ronorm` ("re-read 14:47 under the ZBL rule (§10.13.16)").
- `dens` mode: runs are "compiled, with the fused training kernels off (`DP_TRITON_TRAIN=0 DP_CUDA_TRAIN=0`, the user's rule for the `dens` mode)".
- Corrections at 15:28, 5 September on the inserted norms: for `neo_plain_2gpu_ronorm` and `neo_plain_2gpu_so2post_ronorm`, "a first launch at 15:17 [15:18] with a hard norm (eps 1e-5) and compilation off was stopped at 15:28 on the user's corrections"; the relaunches use eps 1.
- The pre-norm control `h_so2norm_pre` was "stopped 16:05 (the option is slated for removal)".

(No Chinese-language quotations occur in this range.)

#### User decisions — the refreshed reference, §10.13 second half (3–6 September)

No dated decision or constraint attributed to the user appears in lines 3501-3994. The only dated actions in the range are run launches, stops and GPU reassignments (3-5 September 2026), recorded in the experiment table.

#### User decisions — the optimizer and the candidate ledger, §10.14–10.15 (2–6 September)

- **No ZBL, no analytical short-range potential** (15:00, 5 September): E27 and E29 were withdrawn within minutes of launch "on the user's rule that this research must make the model safe without ZBL or any analytical short-range potential (a ZBL version is a separate later phase of theirs)"; no reading was taken. E35/E36 stayed unlaunched because they border the same rule.
- **The ZBL range is exempt** (11:57, 6 September; restated 14:41): the region that counts is outside 0.6 covalent contacts; holes at the scan's inner end are "inside the range ZBL will cover" and are not failures.
- **The two-solution frame and "too complicated"** (14:41, 6 September): "only two kinds of remedy exist — bound the architecture's amplification of the radial signal, or supply data where there is none — and the research had become too complicated and too strict: the Gaussian basis was closed although it delays or displaces the spikes, which is progress in itself …
- **Priorities at extreme short range** (18:10, §12.5): "a softened wall this far inside the contact is an accepted trade-off of a model without ZBL and is not counted as a failure; a hole is"; tears on pairs farther apart than 1 Å weigh most.
- **Try every candidate without asking for rulings** (night of 5 September): E11 was implemented "on the user's instruction of that night to try every candidate without asking for rulings".
- **Stop runs without prospects** (17:25, 4 September for E1; 18:25, 5 September for E28).
- **The observable, not the hyperparameter** (17:20, 5 September): "The user's constraint … is the observable, not the hyperparameter: the dimer scans must show no spurious tail force (criterion A1)" — so E37's soft form, kink-free at m = 0 for any knee, is admissible.
- **Adjust the radial embedding rather than add a pair term** (17:20, 5 September), with E38–E39 placed ahead of E37, and the E35/E36 ruling: "may be tested, but it is symptom-directed — a pair channel answers the dimer while the failure can be many-body — and the radial embedding is the place to act first".
- **Literature note** (17:50, 5 September): "NequIP uses Bessel; eSEN, UMA and EquiformerV2/V3 use Gaussians, 10 on MPTrj/SPICE and 64 on OMat24, and eSEN reports that more radial functions make the surface less smooth" → E40.
- **The proposed final structure** (20:45, 5 September): the Gaussian basis with the SO(2) unit normalized before and after → E42; the standard post-add placement (22:50) → E45.
- **"ffn 的 prenorm 直接去掉呢"** (12:55, 6 September) → E61.
- **The eps-1 layers are not norms** (§10.13.14): "On the eps-1 layers the user is right: … on the data those two layers are learnable constant scalings, not normalisations … From here they are called bounds."
- **AttnRes framing** (4 September): "attnres 只是缓解"，与锚点方法"或许是正交的" → E15.
- **"用 diffusion 的思路"** (4 September) → the hard-contact replay (E6), read as importance sampling of the tails.
- **E64 requested** (13:20, 6 September) as eSEN's count treatment; **E65 requested** (13:47, 6 September): does a different descriptor of the DPA4 family, trained on the same data by the same trainer, show the storm and the holes?
- **Ruled-out method families in this range**: ZBL and pair-scaled zone bridging (E27, E29); the physically-impossible-frame filter and a gradient cap on those frames (E22, E26); the pair-consistency term in every form (E2, E20) with B5 demoted to a diagnostic; direct-force/DeNS pretraining (E21); every attention-residual form alone and on the anchors (E13, E14, E15) …

#### User decisions — the programme, §0 and §11.1–11.26 (6–7 September)

- **6 September 18:00 (§11.7)** — "**The tail term is a reference, not the answer (the user's ruling, 6 September 18:00).** It reaches 4 · 10⁻⁴ eV/Å with no accuracy cost and no displacement of the error into the neighbouring interval, and it was still set aside: a penalty aimed at the quantity the criterion measures is a patch on the symptom, because the freedom that produces the symptom — a response the data never constrain between 4 Å and the cutoff — is left in place." Its OMat24 cells were stopped at 18:03; the hydrogen cells stay "only as the bound that says how far the structural line still has to go".
- **8 September (§0)** — "On 8 September the user decided that the absent elements are not an acceptance criterion: zero-data extrapolation is not a generality question, and anyone needing those elements fine-tunes with data; the scan stays an instrument."
- **Standing exclusions (§11, §0, §11.22)** — "Only genuine normalization is considered; eps-1 amplitude caps and direct penalties on acceptance metrics are excluded." / "The eps-1 caps, direct tail-force penalty, optimizer hyperparameter changes, and analytic short-range potentials are outside the programme." / "no precision or optimizer hyperparameter is changed on this evidence" / "routing the readout to the Adam path is outside the rules unless admitted."
- **§0, repository landing** — "The user decides which of these land in the repository beyond the parts already landed (the `-fix` basis types, the single envelope, the AdamW routing declared by the descriptor)." And: "the pair generator is a research patch, its landing is the user's decision."
- **§0, open question** — "the outer barriers of the molecular pairs, which need the user's decision."
- **§11.3** — "This is the user's own rule of thumb — the force RMSE moves first, the force MAE and the energy follow, and the training loss stays normal — with the isolated-pair function as the leading indicator underneath it." The Max model's validation numbers were measured by the user.

#### User decisions — the structural experiments, §11.27–11.43 (7–8 September)

- 8 Sep, line 1272 — E93 is "**the user's proposal**, not previously trained from scratch": the Bessel basis with the envelope applied once and the radial seed gate, as a 2×2 factorial.
- 8 Sep, line 1363 — "Whether the criteria should require more than boundedness for elements the data do not contain is **the user's decision**; the recipe's goal statement (**"the model may be inaccurate where it has no data; it must not blow up there"**) asks for boundedness only."
- 8 Sep, line 1377 — routing the descriptor readout and fitting weights to the optimizer's Adam path "changes the optimizer and is therefore **outside the rules unless the user admits it**."
- 8 Sep, line 1379 — "The remedies then are **the two the user named**: remove the unconstrained parameter (a basis without functions below the data's floor) or constrain it (anchors that reach the geometry or the type)."
- 8 Sep 10:25, line 1430 — "With **the user's permission of 10:25** the routing becomes an experimental factor", learning rate, clipping, weight decay, dropout and schedule untouched.

#### User decisions — the converged candidate and the isolated-atom limit, §11.44–11.47 (8–11 September)

- **8 Sep 19:05–19:20 — the type axis is not an acceptance criterion.** After the assessment that the
  ten absent elements are a zero-data extrapolation whose bound is a matter of tidiness, the user
  confirmed: "类型轴不作为验收标准，可以". The criteria remain the hand-over's — the 24 isolated pairs'
  tails, holes and false extrema outside the exclusions, the tears, the accuracy budget — and the
  all-element scan is information only.
- **8 Sep 23:05 — the fine-tune scenario must be tested too.** The user reminded the research that
  its evidence is all from pretraining while the deployment path is a fine-tune (Case A, an
  OMat24-pretrained model fine-tuned on the 64-atom hydrogen cells), where in their experience the
  package safeguard already suffices, and asked for the pretraining ingredients to be tried there,
  since the two may differ.
- **9 Sep 13:00 — use UMA's table.** The user chose UMA's isolated-atom references
  (`configs/uma/training_release/element_refs/iso_atom_elem_refs.yaml`, the omat list), the dataset
  authors' own reference, and dropped the MACE-table runs.
- **9 Sep 13:19 and 13:28 — the release configuration, and 16 centres.** The Bessel arm had to use
  the package/release configuration (16 frequencies, not 31), withdrawing the 31-frequency
  submission (job 535) after twelve minutes; then a 16-Gaussian-centre arm was requested.
- **9 Sep 14:05 — revise A1's inner edge.** "4A有点不合理，毕竟物理的肯定才对，或许应该从4.5开始".
- **9 Sep 14:45 — the routing's extent must be evidenced.** The user asked whether the fitting
  network's routing had ever been tested on its own; it had not been on the final basis.
- **9 Sep — the NaN validations.** "nan可能是oom了". Confirmed: the NaN was the evaluation's.
- **9 Sep 19:25 — UMA's appendix G.** After reading appendix G ("Diatomics") of arXiv 2506.23971v2
  the user asked whether the structural work is beside the point and data must be added:
  "我看完以后感觉我们做的这些似乎都意义不大了。。。就是得加数据".
- **11 Sep 10:55 — the fade is rejected, and the basis must be the package's 16 functions.** With
  E147 and E150 (seed 42, 100000) added to the two repository-root dimer figures beside E118, the
  gate-free fixed-Gaussian arm, MACE-OMAT-0 and UMA-s-1p2, the user read the two new curves as
  defective: their energy reaches the isolated-atom limit at about 4 Å and their force carries a bump
  or dip near 4 Å — on H–H a −0.5 eV/Å dip at 4.0 Å below a 0.17 eV secondary well at 3.9 Å inside
  the fade window, on Au–Au a −2 eV/Å dip at 3.8 Å where the fade brings the tail to the limit by
  4.3 Å while MACE's curve is still rising at 6 Å. The judgement: the fade cannot be used, and every
  arm must use the package's 16 radial functions rather than the 31 inherited from E95.
- **11 Sep 11:41 — how the isolated-atom table should reach the model.** Asked how a table could be
  supplied in practice (a hand-written input is out of the question; a per-dataset file leaves
  datasets without tables uncovered), the user proposed putting the information into the dataset
  instead of the model: isolated atoms, and later dimers, as ordinary training frames.
- **11 Sep 12:30 — the data route must be data, not a pipeline change.** The user rejected E158's
  form: "我理解的是直接在数据集中加上孤立原子和dimer，而不是去修改训练pipeline。在数据集中加入以后其他就自然交给dataloader来了".
  E158 was stopped at ≈1000 steps and its patch removed; E159 appends the frames to a copy of the
  LMDB instead. Dimers as data need labels the research does not have, so that half of the proposal
  waits for the user's choice of a label source.
- **Standing constraints and open decisions restated here.** No new labels (§1), which excludes
  computing diatomic curves with OMat24's VASP settings and which the user applied to the isolated
  atoms too; the extreme short range below ≈0.35 contact is inside the future ZBL range and a
  softened surface there is accepted; the outer barrier beyond the well of the molecular pairs
  (§11.37) remains the user's decision; whether the unit-charge bound carries a tolerance for the
  K–F borderline is the user's decision; the reversal of the repository default of routing every
  fitting network to AdamW is the user's decision.

## Appendix A — scripts

Diagnosis (`debug/curvature_reg/diagnose/` and the folder root): `repro_spike.py`, `atom49_pes.py`, `spike_density.py`, `curvature_signal.py`, `locate_amplifier.py`, `frame_continuity.py`, `frame_jump_force.py`, `traj_curvature.py`, `attn_stats.py`, `weight_spectra.py`, `activation_subspace.py`, `attention_response.py`, `needle_geometry.py`, `tear_replay.py`, `tear_amplitude.py`, `message_response.py`, `anchor_response.py`, `radial_response.py`, `seed_response.py`, `curvature_split.py`, `radial_row_drift.py`, `shell_response.py`, `trimer_probe.py`, `increment_probe.py`, `needle_probe.py`, `gain_probe.py`.

Instruments: `dimer_survey.py` (24-pair and all-element surveys with the revised tail fields), `dimer_scan.py`, `pro_scan_fast.py` and `pro_scan.py` (validation and probe scans), `eval_model.py`, `h_accuracy.py`, `matrix_accuracy.sh`, `read_matrix.py`, `needle_window.py`, `pair_window_read.py`, `external_survey.py` (MACE and UMA on the programme's instruments), `contact_anchor_probe.py`, `hard_contact_series.py`, `shortrange_scan.py`, `compress_probe.py`, `pair_contact_stats.py`, `anchor_reach.py`, `h_contact_audit.py`, `subset_lmdb.py`, `append_isolated_atoms.py`.

Training patches (monkey patches on `dp --pt train`): `train_curv.py` (curvature penalty, early prototype of the anchors), `train_refresh.py` (the reference term with hand-over and every ledger option by flag), `radial_experiment.py`, `message_envelope.py`, `attention_normalization.py`, `edge_type_neighbors.py`, `seed_radial_gate.py`, `outer_radial_gate.py`, `pair_fade.py`, `isolated_reference.py`, `adam_route.py`, `fitting_normalization.py`, `plain_fitting.py`, `reference_loss.py`, `original_reference.py`, `cluster_anchors.py`, `pair_anchors.py`, `replay_anchors.py`, `heal_spike.py`, `heal_pro.py`, `muon_ridge.py`.

Launchers and chains: `run_recipe.py`, `acceptance_chain.sh`, `continue_run.sh`, `instruments_watch.sh`, `watch_run.py`, `slurm_h.sh`, `slurm_twin.sh`, the dated launch scripts under `runs/`, and the launch record `runs/launch_acceptance_20260908.json` with every experiment's runs, factors and Slurm job.

## Appendix B — data and checkpoints

`temp/normal/` (the failed hydrogen run and its archived frames), `temp/model_ema.ckpt.pt` (the hydrogen model), `temp/H_data/` (its training and validation data); `/data/Datasets/LMDB/OMat24/{trn,val,sub01val,sub001val}.lmdb` (OMat24 and its validation subsets, replicated on every node); `/nas/outisli/Datasets/LMDB/OMat24_iso/trn.lmdb` (OMat24 with 186900 isolated-atom frames, E159); `runs/pro_ckpts/` (DPA4-Pro checkpoints 200k–806k), `runs/neo_plain/`, `runs/neo_safe/` (the production pair), `runs/data/H64_zbl/` (hydrogen relabelled with a ZBL wall); `runs/uma_omat_isolated_atom_energies.json` and `runs/mace_omat0_isolated_atom_energies.json` (89 elements each); every experiment's directory under `runs/<run>/` with `experiment.json`, `lcurve.out`, `batch_max_error_rank0.log`, the surveys `survey_full_<step>.json`, `homo_scan/`, `trimer_probe.jsonl` and the EMA checkpoints; the reference-model scans in the session scratchpad and the two figures `scan_h_h.png`, `scan_au_au.png` in the repository root.
